# Темы, утверждения и формулы

[Главный индекс](README.md) · [Словарь](GLOSSARY.md) · [Оглавление всей книги](BOOK-INDEX.md) · [Карта страниц](PAGE-MAP.md)

Здесь индексируется **обработанный** основной текст и приложения: печатные с. 1–764 / PDF 37–800. Главы 1–19 и приложения A–B завершены. Тип и условия исходного утверждения сохраняются; редакторские проверки доступны отдельно. В частности, Proposition 18.4, Lemma 19.6 и Proposition 19.9 не следует применять без разбора оговорок. Авторские экспериментальные сообщения и синтетические иллюстрации отмечены раздельно.

| [8.4](source/0285-0292-ch08-reextraction-and-residual-algebra.md#remark-8-4) | Remark: Genericity versus trained checkpoints | Generic rank — утверждение об исключительном множестве, не о численном ранге данного checkpoint. | 255 / 291 |

| [8.10](source/0293-0300-ch08-reextraction-cost-transport-and-entropy.md#remark-8-10) | Remark: Metric and coupling dependence | При фиксированной метрике tie-break не меняет оптимальную стоимость; смена метрики меняет W2. | 258 / 294 |
| [8.11](source/0293-0300-ch08-reextraction-cost-transport-and-entropy.md#proposition-8-11) | Proposition: Qualitative cone–SVD correspondence | Утверждение о centered SVD и cone index неверно без дополнительных условий; контрпримеры в карточке 77. | 261 / 297 |

| [8.13](source/0293-0300-ch08-reextraction-cost-transport-and-entropy.md#remark-8-13) | Remark: Endpoint values | При нулевом margin верхняя граница log n; равномерность распределения из этого не следует. | 262 / 298 |
| [8.14](source/0293-0300-ch08-reextraction-cost-transport-and-entropy.md#proposition-8-14) | Proposition: Conditional decay of the entropy proxy | Построчное увеличение margin уменьшает верхнюю границу; фактическая энтропия может увеличиться. | 264 / 300 |
| [8.15](source/0293-0300-ch08-reextraction-cost-transport-and-entropy.md#remark-8-15) | Remark: Conditional interpretation | Условная интерпретация профиля, не универсальная гарантия поведения обучения. | 264 / 300 |

| [8.19](source/0301-0305-ch08-compatibility-bounds-and-signature-transport.md#remark-8-19) | Remark: Status of the theorem | Автор сам отделяет измеряемые предпосылки от универсального закона монотонности. | 267 / 303 |

### Ключевые ненумерованные формулы

| Формула / объект | Где найти | Что сохранить при использовании |
|---|---|---|
| $E(v)=e_{\iota(v)}^\top W_E$ | [1.2](source/0037-0043-ch01-embeddings-and-sinusoidal.md#definition-1-2) | Токены — строки таблицы. |
| $L=XMX^\top$ | [1.6](source/0037-0043-ch01-embeddings-and-sinusoidal.md#definition-1-6) | Размер $n\times n$; $M$ может быть несимметричной. |
| Перестановка входа и выхода | [1.8](source/0037-0043-ch01-embeddings-and-sinusoidal.md#theorem-1-8), [матричное разъяснение](notes/02-order-and-sinusoidal-encoding.md) | Условия механизма и различие эквивариантности с инвариантностью. |
| $\omega_k=10000^{-2k/d}$; sin/cos | [1.9](source/0037-0043-ch01-embeddings-and-sinusoidal.md#definition-1-9) | Позиции с 1, пары координат с 0; исправление предельной частоты находится в заметке. |
| $\mathrm{PE}_i\cdot\mathrm{PE}_j=\sum_k\cos(\omega_k(i-j))$ | [1.10(ii)](source/0037-0043-ch01-embeddings-and-sinusoidal.md#proposition-1-10) | Равенство точное для скалярного произведения. |
| Четыре члена score при добавлении позиции | [PDF 44](source/0044-0050-ch01-trainable-and-statistical-position.md#pdf-0044) | Token–token, token–position, position–token, position–position. |
| $\bar e_i=\mathbb E_{v\sim\mu_i}[E(v)]$ | [PDF 44](source/0044-0050-ch01-trainable-and-statistical-position.md#pdf-0044) | $E$ фиксировано в рассматриваемом аргументе. |
| Антисимметричное возмущение строк $p_i,p_j$ | [1.11](source/0044-0050-ch01-trainable-and-statistical-position.md#lemma-1-11) | Аргумент первого порядка при $\mathcal L\in C^1$. |
| $L_{ij}^{\mathrm{RoPE}}=q_iR(\omega(i-j))k_j^\top$ | [PDF 49](source/0044-0050-ch01-trainable-and-statistical-position.md#pdf-0049) | Относительной является позиционная зависимость; содержание остаётся в $q_i,k_j$. |
| $\mu_i(v)=\Pr[t_i=v]$ | [§1.3.5](source/0044-0050-ch01-trainable-and-statistical-position.md#section-1-3-5) | Маргинальная статистика позиции по корпусу. |
| $P=AB^\top$; $r(n+d)$ | [PDF 49](source/0044-0050-ch01-trainable-and-statistical-position.md#pdf-0049) | В [подписи таблицы 1.1, PDF 33](source/0033-0036-list-of-tables.md#pdf-0033) указаны $n=32,d=128$, что объясняет $160r$. [Таблица на PDF 55](source/0051-0063-ch01-geometry-normalisation-and-residuals.md#pdf-0055) обработана; полный ранг r=31 в указанной факторизации требует 4960 параметров, больше 4096. |
| $d_H=\lVert\Phi(\mu)-\Phi(\nu)\rVert_2$ | [1.17](source/0044-0050-ch01-trainable-and-statistical-position.md#lemma-1-17) | $\Phi$ — покомпонентный квадратный корень; диапазон $[0,\sqrt2]$. |
| $d_{\mathrm{FR}}=2\arccos\sum_v\sqrt{\mu(v)\nu(v)}$ | [1.18](source/0044-0050-ch01-trainable-and-statistical-position.md#remark-1-18) | Сохранить коэффициент 2; не заменять хордой. |
| W1-дрейф распределений сигнатур | [PDF 251](source/0251-0257-ch07-signature-transport-and-closed-locus.md#definition-7-14) | Метрика и фиксированный словарь; отсутствие канонического head lineage. |
| Margin → точная построчная entropy upper bound | [PDF 253](source/0251-0257-ch07-signature-transport-and-closed-locus.md#theorem-7-18) | Построчный margin; нельзя автоматически заменить его средним. |
| Дифференциал MHA и Jacobian | [PDF 255](source/0251-0257-ch07-signature-transport-and-closed-locus.md#proposition-7-20) | Все value/output-множители; контекст изолированного блока. |
| Первое направление проекции около uniform | [PDF 257](source/0251-0257-ch07-signature-transport-and-closed-locus.md#proposition-7-24) | Row-centering logits и направленная производная выпуклой проекции. |

| [Рисунок 7.14](source/0258-0264-ch07-training-dynamics-and-geography-pipeline.md#figure-7-14) | The signature pipeline. Layer geography is the empirical distribution of head signatures. | Книга 228 / PDF 264 |
| [Рисунок 7.15](source/0265-0267-ch07-replacement-map-and-hypotheses.md#figure-7-15) | Toy residual examples. Canonical atoms and exact mixtures have zero residual. | [Рисунок 7.17](source/0277-0284-ch07-metric-geometry-and-canonical-replacement.md#figure-7-17) | The XIA stack. Local signatures become metric objects, empirical measures, transport curves, dynamical trajectories, and finally intervention targets. | Книга 244 / PDF 280 |
| [Рисунок 7.18](source/0277-0284-ch07-metric-geometry-and-canonical-replacement.md#figure-7-18) | Canonical replacement. Each attention map is replaced by its canonical projection. | [Рисунок 8.1](source/0285-0292-ch08-reextraction-and-residual-algebra.md#figure-8-1) | Synthetic layer geography used throughout the explanatory experiments. | Книга 251 / PDF 287 |
| [Рисунок 8.2](source/0285-0292-ch08-reextraction-and-residual-algebra.md#figure-8-2) | The signature re-extraction loop. A mixed residual representation is passed to the next block. Local attention maps and their signatures are extracted again from that representation. Thus the layer geography is regenerated at each depth. | Книга 254 / PDF 290 |
| [Рисунок 8.3](source/0285-0292-ch08-reextraction-and-residual-algebra.md#figure-8-3) | Head-gauge symmetry. A joint permutation of head outputs and output-projection blocks leaves the sum unchanged. The empirical signature distribution is invariant under this relabelling, while the individual head labels are not. | Книга 255 / PDF 291 |
| [Рисунок 8.4](source/0285-0292-ch08-reextraction-and-residual-algebra.md#figure-8-4) | Score-side gauge structure. Different query–key factorisations can determine the same canonical pair $M=W_QW_K^\top$. The labelled head index is gauge-dependent, while the multiset of canonical pairs and the induced signature geography are the objects used by the chapter. | Книга 256 / PDF 292 |
| [Рисунок 8.5](source/0293-0300-ch08-reextraction-cost-transport-and-entropy.md#figure-8-5) | Synthetic re-extraction cost profile. The cost is high when consecutive layers use unrelated canonical QK-pair populations, and it decreases when the score-side geometry stabilises. This diagnostic is distinct from, but complementary to, Wasserstein drift in signature space. | Книга 258 / PDF 294 |
| [Рисунок 8.6](source/0293-0300-ch08-reextraction-cost-transport-and-entropy.md#figure-8-6) | Synthetic velocity profile $v_\ell=W_2(\mu_\ell,\mu_{\ell+1})$. Large peaks indicate transition layers in which the signature geography changes rapidly. Small values indicate near-conservative transport. | Книга 259 / PDF 295 |
| [Рисунок 8.7](source/0293-0300-ch08-reextraction-cost-transport-and-entropy.md#figure-8-7) | Synthetic optimal coupling between two consecutive layer geographies. The plot yields a best geometric matching under the chosen signature metric. No canonical identity statement follows from the heat map; the coupling is a diagnostic surrogate rather than a structural head correspondence. | Книга 260 / PDF 296 |
| [Рисунок 8.8](source/0293-0300-ch08-reextraction-cost-transport-and-entropy.md#figure-8-8) | Distance from a canonical vertex as a small amount of mass is moved away from the vertex. Hellinger and $\ell_1$ encode different local geometries. Near the boundary, the square-root geometry produces a sharper metric sensitivity to specialised heads. | Книга 261 / PDF 297 |
| [Рисунок 8.9](source/0293-0300-ch08-reextraction-cost-transport-and-entropy.md#figure-8-9) | Synthetic relation between the cone index and the dominant singular-mass ratio. The figure is illustrative rather than empirical. Its purpose is to make explicit the interpretation of $I_{\mathrm{cone}}$ as a coarse observable of singular alignment. | Книга 262 / PDF 298 |
| [Рисунок 8.10](source/0293-0300-ch08-reextraction-cost-transport-and-entropy.md#figure-8-10) | Synthetic entropy–margin scatter. The solid curve is the theoretical upper bound from Theorem 8.12. The plot illustrates the chapter’s main point: margin growth sharpens attention concentration, but the exact entropy trajectory still depends on the detailed row distribution. | Книга 263 / PDF 299 |
| [Рисунок 8.11](source/0301-0305-ch08-compatibility-bounds-and-signature-transport.md#figure-8-11) | Synthetic transition observables. $\Phi_\ell^\Lambda$ measures mean barycentric transport, $\Phi_\ell^\rho$ measures mean residual change, and $\Delta\operatorname{Spread}_\ell$ measures concentration or diversification of the signature cloud. Large values identify transitions in which the next layer materially changes the extracted geometry. | Книга 265 / PDF 301 |
| [Рисунок 8.12](source/0301-0305-ch08-compatibility-bounds-and-signature-transport.md#figure-8-12) | Compatibility profile. Cone alignment, directional activation, margins, entropy, and residual coordinate each measure a different aspect of re-extraction. The arrows indicate diagnostic compatibility, not an unconditional causal chain. | Книга 266 / PDF 302 |
| [Рисунок 9.1](source/0312-0317-ch09-motor-taxonomy-and-related-work.md#figure-9-1) | Taxonomy of motors. A signal motor carries variation, a structural motor shapes variation, and an effective motor is the composed object that actually acts on the variable of interest. | Книга 277 / PDF 313 |
| [Рисунок 9.2](source/0318-0324-ch09-mlm-graph-feedforward-and-softmax.md#figure-9-2) | Computational graph of one BERT encoder block at masked position $m$. | Книга 283 / PDF 319 |
| [Рисунок 9.3](source/0318-0324-ch09-mlm-graph-feedforward-and-softmax.md#figure-9-3) | Differential anatomy of one encoder block at a masked position. The task motor is pulled back through the feed-forward and multi-head-attention branches, while the residual stream carries a direct motor. The MHA contribution is split into value, coefficient, score, query, and key paths. | Книга 285 / PDF 321 |
| [Рисунок 9.4](source/0318-0324-ch09-mlm-graph-feedforward-and-softmax.md#figure-9-4) | Simplified differential anatomy of one masked-position BERT block. The primary signal motor enters through the MLM head, is gated by the MLP, and is split into headwise Q/K/V motors inside self-attention. | Книга 286 / PDF 322 |
| [Рисунок 9.5](source/0318-0324-ch09-mlm-graph-feedforward-and-softmax.md#figure-9-5) | Synthetic ReLU gate sparsity. The gate is a structural motor: it determines which hidden channels transmit the incoming signal. | Книга 287 / PDF 323 |
| [Рисунок 9.6](source/0318-0324-ch09-mlm-graph-feedforward-and-softmax.md#figure-9-6) | Softmax as a simplex-constrained structural motor. The Jacobian projects variations into the tangent space of the probability simplex and annihilates the all-ones direction. | Книга 288 / PDF 324 |
| [Рисунок 9.7](source/0325-0329-ch09-headwise-recursion-and-score-motors.md#figure-9-7) | Synthetic headwise Q/K/V motor-energy split. The figure illustrates that different heads may carry different proportions of query, key, and value motor energy. | Книга 290 / PDF 326 |
| [Рисунок 9.8](source/0330-0334-ch09-sa-maps-and-low-rank-motors.md#figure-9-8) | Symmetric–antisymmetric splitting of the score motor. The motor on $M=W_QW_K^\top$ is projected onto the symmetric and antisymmetric subspaces. The two projected flows are orthogonal and must both be kept when global BERT/SARS pattern maps are analysed. | Книга 294 / PDF 330 |
| [Рисунок 9.9](source/0330-0334-ch09-sa-maps-and-low-rank-motors.md#figure-9-9) | BERT/SARS global S–A balance map. Each cell is a layer–head pair and records the antisymmetric fraction $\alpha_{\ell,h}$. The map is the score-side companion of the signature-geography maps: it shows whether a head is primarily reciprocal, mixed, or directional before row-wise softmax and canonical projection. | Книга 295 / PDF 331 |
| [Рисунок 9.10](source/0330-0334-ch09-sa-maps-and-low-rank-motors.md#figure-9-10) | BERT/SARS S–A regime map. The continuous balance $\alpha_{\ell,h}$ is coarse-grained into symmetric, mixed, and antisymmetric regimes. This coarse map should not replace the continuous map; it is useful for visualising global layer–head organisation and for comparing with coarse signature regimes. | Книга 295 / PDF 331 |
| [Рисунок 9.11](source/0330-0334-ch09-sa-maps-and-low-rank-motors.md#figure-9-11) | Layer-wise S–A and residual profiles. The symmetric and antisymmetric energies give complementary forward score-side observables, while the canonical residual monitors how much of the resulting attention behaviour is captured by the signature dictionary. Peaks and crossings are candidate transition regions for Chapters 10–11. | Книга 296 / PDF 332 |
| [Рисунок 9.12](source/0330-0334-ch09-sa-maps-and-low-rank-motors.md#figure-9-12) | S–A balance as a predictor of signature mass. The plot illustrates the expected bridge: larger antisymmetric fraction should correlate with directional signature mass, while lower antisymmetric fraction should correlate with reciprocal or symmetric signature mass. The exact relationship is mediated by softmax, input geometry, and canonical residual. | Книга 297 / PDF 333 |
| [Рисунок 9.13](source/0335-0341-ch09-motor-rank-anisotropy-and-bounds.md#figure-9-13) | Synthetic accumulation of local outer-product motor terms. The exact rank grows at most linearly with the number of terms, while the effective rank may remain lower when terms are correlated. | Книга 299 / PDF 335 |
| [Рисунок 9.14](source/0335-0341-ch09-motor-rank-anisotropy-and-bounds.md#figure-9-14) | Synthetic verification that the softmax Jacobian annihilates the all-ones direction. Numerical values concentrate near machine precision. | Книга 300 / PDF 336 |
| [Рисунок 9.15](source/0335-0341-ch09-motor-rank-anisotropy-and-bounds.md#figure-9-15) | Value-path anisotropy. A head motor aligned with a dominant direction of the value cloud produces high anisotropy; a motor spread across many value directions produces lower anisotropy. | Книга 302 / PDF 338 |

## Точная граница прочитанного

Обработаны **PDF 1–800 из 818**: все 36 вводных страниц, главы 1–19 и приложения A–B полностью. Глава 18 заканчивается на PDF 762 / книге 726, глава 19 — на PDF 791 / книге 755; приложение A занимает PDF 792–796 / книгу 756–760, приложение B — PDF 797–800 / книгу 761–764. PDF 800 завершает §B.8; разрыва предложения на границе пакета нет.
